23:["$","section","Actions",{"className":"flex flex-col gap-3","children":[["$","$L29",null,{"id":"note-actions","children":"Actions"}],["$","div",null,{"className":"typeset typeset-docs text-muted-foreground","children":["$","p",null,{"children":["$","$L33",null,{"text":"With `actions` the item is a list item holding the row button and the actions side by side, so a menu button never nests inside the row and its clicks don't reach the row. The actions show on hover, keyboard focus, while the row is active and while a menu they opened is open; on touch screens they always show and `meta` moves left of them. Then `className`, `id`, `style` and `data-*` go to the list item, and everything else (`onClick`, `aria-*`, `disabled`) to the row button; `buttonProps` reaches the button directly. Without `actions` the button is the item and takes everything."}]}]}]]}]

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ThreadListItemProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"button"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"title"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Provider / agent glyph shown before the title. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Repository or project name. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  repo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Linked pull request; its number takes the status colour. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  pr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> PullRequestStatus</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  branch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Agent is working: the icon becomes a spinner. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  running</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** New activity since last opened: a dot after the title. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  unread</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Trailing content inside the row button. Put interactive controls in `actions` instead. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  trailing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Free second line (a subtitle, a brief); truncates. Shown above `repo` / `pr` / `branch`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  description</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Quiet text at the end of the title line, such as a relative time. `actions` replace it on hover. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  meta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * Controls for the row (a "⋯" menu), placed over its end outside the row button. Shown on hover,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * keyboard focus, while active or while a menu they opened is open; always shown on touch screens.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  actions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * Props for the row button. Without `actions` the button is the root, so this merges with the rest.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * With `actions`, `className`, `id`, `style` and `data-*` go to the list item and everything else</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * (`onClick`, `aria-*`, `disabled`) to the button; use this for anything else the button needs.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  buttonProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"button"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Two-line thread row: provider icon and title, then a description or the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * repo, pull request number in its status colour, and branch, all truncating</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * so the branch gives way first. With `actions` the row becomes a list item</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * holding the row button and the actions side by side, so no button nests in another.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ThreadListItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  repo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  pr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  branch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  running</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  unread</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  trailing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  description</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  meta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  actions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  buttonProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ThreadListItemProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">labels</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> useLocale</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> refs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> repo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> pr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> branch</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> hasActions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> actions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> actions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // With actions the row is a list item around the button: identity and layout go to the item.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> itemProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Record</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">unknown</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> rowProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Record</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">unknown</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">k</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">v</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">entries</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(props)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (hasActions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (k </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> k </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "style"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> k.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">startsWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"data-"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">))) itemProps[k] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> v</span></span>
